#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+    // Keep the team alive across pivots; the worksharing barrier completes
+    // each pivot before any thread starts the next one.
+    #pragma omp parallel

+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                // With nonnegative weights and a zero diagonal, the pivot row
+                // cannot improve. Leaving it read-only also avoids data races.
+                if (i == k) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
+
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
+                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+                    if (newDist < row[j]) {
+                        row[j] = newDist;
+                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
+                    }
                 }
             }
         }

codex
The first version matches the original hashes and scales from 2,212 ms on one thread to 58 ms on 64 threads at 2,048 nodes. I’m testing whether batching pivots can improve cache reuse and reduce synchronization while preserving the exact path updates.

/bin/bash -lc 'cat floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp' in /home/llmtest/evals/20261006-204623/floydwarshall_gpt-6.1-sol-xhigh_omp_r4
 succeeded in 0ms:
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        unsigned int* row = path.data() + i * numNodes;
        #pragma omp simd
        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
            row[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(i);
        }
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    unsigned int* distances = dist.data();
    unsigned int* paths = path.data();

    // Keep the team alive across pivots; the worksharing barrier completes
    // each pivot before any thread starts the next one.
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
            const unsigned int* pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
            const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);

            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
                // With nonnegative weights and a zero diagonal, the pivot row
                // cannot improve. Leaving it read-only also avoids data races.
                if (i == k) {
                    continue;
                }
                unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
                unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];

                #pragma omp simd
                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
                    if (newDist < row[j]) {
                        row[j] = newDist;
                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

/bin/bash -lc "g++ -x c++ -std=c++20 -O3 -march=native -fopenmp -Wall -Wextra -I floydwarshall -o /tmp/floydwarshall-equivalence - <<'CPP'
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"

constexpr unsigned int reference_INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int reference_MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t reference_idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void reference_initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[reference_idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void reference_initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[reference_idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[reference_idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[reference_idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void reference_floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[reference_idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[reference_idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[reference_idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[reference_idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[reference_idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool reference_validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[reference_idx2(i, i, numNodes)] "'!= 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero'"\\n\", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n"'^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[reference_idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[reference_idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[reference_idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < reference_INF && distKJ < reference_INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]'"\\n\", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void reference_printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);
    printf(\"Options:\\n\");
    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");
}

int reference_main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {
            reference_printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);
            reference_printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");
    printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);
    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");
    reference_initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, reference_MAX_DISTANCE);
    reference_initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    reference_floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, \"DistanceMatrix\");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");
        bool valid = reference_validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#define main parallel_main
#include \"floydwarshall.cpp\"
#undef main
#include <random>
int main() {
    std::mt19937 rng(12345);
    size_t cases = 0;
    for (int threads : {1, 2, 3, 4, 8, 16}) {
        omp_set_num_threads(threads);
        for (size_t n : {0, 1, 2, 3, 7, 15, 16, 17, 31, 32, 33, 63, 64, 65, 127, 128, 129, 255, 256, 257, 512, 513}) {
            for (int mode = 0; mode < 3; ++mode) {
                std::vector<unsigned int> expected(n*n), expectedPath(n*n);
                if (mode == 0) {
                    reference_initializeDistanceMatrix(expected, n, 1, reference_MAX_DISTANCE);
                } else {
                    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
                        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
                            expected[i*n+j] = i == j ? 0 : (mode == 1 ? rng()%11 : (rng()%4 == 0 ? reference_INF : rng()%1000));
                        }
                    }
                }
                reference_initializePathMatrix(expectedPath, n);
                auto actual = expected;
                std::vector<unsigned int> actualPath(n*n);
                initializePathMatrix(actualPath, n);
                if (expectedPath "'!= actualPath) {
                    std::fprintf(stderr, "Initialization mismatch: n=%zu threads=%d'"\\n\", n, threads);
                    return 1;
                }
                reference_floydWarshall(expected, expectedPath, n);
                floydWarshall(actual, actualPath, n);
                if (actual "'!= expected || actualPath != expectedPath) {
                    for (size_t q = 0; q < n*n; ++q) {
                        if (actual[q] != expected[q] || actualPath[q] != expectedPath[q]) {
                            std::fprintf(stderr, "Mismatch: n=%zu threads=%d mode=%d cell=%zu dist=%u/%u path=%u/%u'"\\n\",
                                n, threads, mode, q, actual[q], expected[q], actualPath[q], expectedPath[q]);
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    return 1;
                }
                ++cases;
            }
        }
    }
    std::printf(\"PASS: exact distance and path equality in %zu cases\\n\", cases);
}

/bin/bash -lc "g++ -x c++ -std=c++20 -O3 -march=native -fopenmp -I floydwarshall -o /tmp/floydwarshall-rows - <<'CPP'
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        unsigned int* row = path.data() + i * numNodes;
        #pragma omp simd
        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
            row[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(i);
        }
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    unsigned int* distances = dist.data();
    unsigned int* paths = path.data();

    // Keep the team alive across pivots; the worksharing barrier completes
    // each pivot before any thread starts the next one.
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
            const unsigned int* pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
            const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);

            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
                // With nonnegative weights and a zero diagonal, the pivot row
                // cannot improve. Leaving it read-only also avoids data races.
                if (i == k) {
                    continue;
                }
                unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
                unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];

                #pragma omp simd
                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
                    if (newDist < row[j]) {
                        row[j] = newDist;
                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] "'!= 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero'"\\n\", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n"'^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]'"\\n\", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);
    printf(\"Options:\\n\");
    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");
    printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);
    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, \"DistanceMatrix\");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#include <omp.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        unsigned int* row = path.data() + i * numNodes;
        #pragma omp simd
        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
            row[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(i);
        }
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    unsigned int* distances = dist.data();
    unsigned int* paths = path.data();

    constexpr size_t maxPivots = 32;
    constexpr size_t columnTile = 256;
    const size_t pivotBatch = std::min(maxPivots, std::max(size_t{1},
        numNodes / (4 * static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()))));
    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotStorage(pivotBatch * numNodes);
    unsigned int* pivots = pivotStorage.data();

    // Snapshot successive pivot rows so each source row can process a batch
    // independently, while still applying every pivot in its original order.
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        for (size_t first = 0; first < numNodes; first += pivotBatch) {
            const size_t last = std::min(first + pivotBatch, numNodes);

            #pragma omp single
            {
                for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
                    unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
                    std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes, numNodes, pivot);
                    for (size_t i = first; i < last; ++i) {
                        if (i == k) {
                            continue;
                        }
                        unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
                        unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
                        const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
                        #pragma omp simd
                        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                            const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
                            if (newDist < row[j]) {
                                row[j] = newDist;
                                pathRow[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
                if (i >= first && i < last) {
                    continue;
                }
                unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
                unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
                unsigned int distToPivot[maxPivots];

                // These columns supply the source-to-pivot distances for all
                // other tiles. Save each value before applying that pivot.
                for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
                    const unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
                    const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
                    distToPivot[k - first] = distIK;
                    #pragma omp simd
                    for (size_t j = first; j < last; ++j) {
                        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
                        if (newDist < row[j]) {
                            row[j] = newDist;
                            pathRow[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
                        }
                    }
                }

                for (size_t column = 0; column < numNodes;) {
                    if (column == first) {
                        column = last;
                        continue;
                    }
                    const size_t columnEnd = std::min(column + columnTile,
                        column < first ? first : numNodes);
                    for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
                        const unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
                        const unsigned int distIK = distToPivot[k - first];
                        #pragma omp simd
                        for (size_t j = column; j < columnEnd; ++j) {
                            const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
                            if (newDist < row[j]) {
                                row[j] = newDist;
                                pathRow[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    column = columnEnd;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

 void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                    std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                    const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+    unsigned int* distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* paths = path.data();
+
+    constexpr size_t maxPivots = 32;
+    constexpr size_t columnTile = 256;
+    const size_t pivotBatch = std::min(maxPivots, std::max(size_t{1},
+        numNodes / (4 * static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()))));
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotStorage(pivotBatch * numNodes);
+    unsigned int* pivots = pivotStorage.data();
+
+    // Snapshot successive pivot rows so each source row can process a batch
+    // independently, while still applying every pivot in its original order.
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        for (size_t first = 0; first < numNodes; first += pivotBatch) {
+            const size_t last = std::min(first + pivotBatch, numNodes);
+
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                    unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
+                    std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes, numNodes, pivot);
+                    for (size_t i = first; i < last; ++i) {
+                        if (i == k) {
+                            continue;
+                        }
+                        unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                        unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                        const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
+                        #pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
+                            const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+                            if (newDist < row[j]) {
+                                row[j] = newDist;
+                                pathRow[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                if (i >= first && i < last) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int distToPivot[maxPivots];
+
+                // These columns supply the source-to-pivot distances for all
+                // other tiles. Save each value before applying that pivot.
+                for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                    const unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
+                    const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
+                    distToPivot[k - first] = distIK;
+                    #pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t j = first; j < last; ++j) {
+                        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+                        if (newDist < row[j]) {
+                            row[j] = newDist;
+                            pathRow[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t column = 0; column < numNodes;) {
+                    if (column == first) {
+                        column = last;
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    const size_t columnEnd = std::min(column + columnTile,
+                        column < first ? first : numNodes);
+                    for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                        const unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
+                        const unsigned int distIK = distToPivot[k - first];
+                        #pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t j = column; j < columnEnd; ++j) {
+                            const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+                            if (newDist < row[j]) {
+                                row[j] = newDist;
+                                pathRow[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                    column = columnEnd;
                 }
             }

codex
Both matrices match the serial code exactly in 396 tests, including irregular sizes, zero-weight edges, and disconnected graphs. Batching helps larger graphs—8,192 nodes fell from 24.3 s to 9.2 s on 16 threads—but adds overhead on smaller graphs. I’ll use those measurements to choose when it applies.

/bin/bash -lc "g++ -x c++ -std=c++20 -O3 -march=native -fopenmp -I floydwarshall -o /tmp/floydwarshall-pruned - <<'CPP'
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>
#include <limits>
#include <omp.h>

#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        unsigned int* row = path.data() + i * numNodes;
        #pragma omp simd
        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
            row[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(i);
        }
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    unsigned int* distances = dist.data();
    unsigned int* paths = path.data();

    std::vector<unsigned int> rowMaxima(numNodes);
    unsigned int minimumEdge = std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max();

    // Keep the team alive across pivots; the worksharing barrier completes
    // each pivot before any thread starts the next one.
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        #pragma omp for schedule(static) reduction(min:minimumEdge)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            unsigned int rowMaximum = 0;
            const unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                rowMaximum = std::max(rowMaximum, row[j]);
                if (j "'!= i) {
                    minimumEdge = std::min(minimumEdge, row[j]);
                }
            }
            rowMaxima[i] = rowMaximum;
        }
        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
            const unsigned int* pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
            const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);

            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
                // With nonnegative weights and a zero diagonal, the pivot row
                // cannot improve. Leaving it read-only also avoids data races.
                if (i == k) {
                    continue;
                }
                unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
                unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
                if (distIK + minimumEdge >= rowMaxima[i]) {
                    continue;
                }
                unsigned int rowMaximum = 0;

                #pragma omp simd reduction(max:rowMaximum)
                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
                    if (newDist < row[j]) {
                        row[j] = newDist;
                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
                    }
                    rowMaximum = std::max(rowMaximum, row[j]);
                }
                rowMaxima[i] = rowMaximum;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero'"\\n\", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n"'^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]'"\\n\", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);
    printf(\"Options:\\n\");
    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");
    printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);
    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, \"DistanceMatrix\");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

CPP
