OMP_PLACES=cores OMP_PROC_BIND=close /tmp/floydwarshall-final-equivalence" in /home/llmtest/evals/20261006-204623/floydwarshall_gpt-6.1-sol-xhigh_omp_r4
exec
/bin/bash -lc "g++ -x c++ -std=c++20 -O1 -g -fopenmp -fsanitize=address,undefined -fno-omit-frame-pointer -I floydwarshall -o /tmp/floydwarshall-sanitized - <<'CPP'
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"

constexpr unsigned int reference_INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int reference_MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t reference_idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void reference_initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[reference_idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void reference_initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[reference_idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[reference_idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[reference_idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void reference_floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[reference_idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[reference_idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[reference_idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[reference_idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[reference_idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool reference_validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[reference_idx2(i, i, numNodes)] "'!= 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero'"\\n\", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n"'^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[reference_idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[reference_idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[reference_idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < reference_INF && distKJ < reference_INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]'"\\n\", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void reference_printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);
    printf(\"Options:\\n\");
    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");
}

int reference_main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {
            reference_printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);
            reference_printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");
    printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);
    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");
    reference_initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, reference_MAX_DISTANCE);
    reference_initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    reference_floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, \"DistanceMatrix\");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");
        bool valid = reference_validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#define main parallel_main
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include <omp.h>

#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        unsigned int* row = path.data() + i * numNodes;
        #pragma omp simd
        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
            row[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(i);
        }
    }
}

// Every destination in a row is independent for a fixed pivot. The caller
// supplies an immutable pivot row and the source-to-pivot distance.
inline void updateDistanceRow(unsigned int* row, unsigned int* pathRow,
                              const unsigned int* pivot, const unsigned int distIK,
                              const unsigned int intermediate,
                              const size_t begin, const size_t end) {
    #pragma omp simd
    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
        if (newDist < row[j]) {
            row[j] = newDist;
            pathRow[j] = intermediate;
        }
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    unsigned int* distances = dist.data();
    unsigned int* paths = path.data();

    constexpr size_t maxPivots = 32;
    constexpr size_t columnTile = 256;
    const size_t maxThreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
    // Batching saves cache traffic for large row partitions. Smaller ones
    // benefit from direct pivots, without snapshotting or serial preparation.
    const bool useBatches = true;
    const size_t pivotBatch = useBatches
        ? std::min(maxPivots, std::max(size_t{1}, numNodes / (4 * maxThreads)))
        : 1;
    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotStorage(useBatches ? pivotBatch * numNodes : 0);
    unsigned int* pivots = pivotStorage.data();

    // Reuse one team for the entire computation. Static row ownership keeps
    // writes disjoint and makes successive updates reuse the same caches.
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        if ("'!useBatches) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int* pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
                #pragma omp for schedule(static)
                for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
                    // Nonnegative weights and a zero diagonal mean this row
                    // never improves. Keep it read-only for the whole team.
                    if (i == k) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
                    updateDistanceRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot, row[k],
                                      static_cast<unsigned int>(k), 0, numNodes);
                }
                // The implicit barrier completes this pivot before the next.
            }
        } else {
            for (size_t first = 0; first < numNodes; first += pivotBatch) {
                const size_t last = std::min(first + pivotBatch, numNodes);

                // Prepare only the source rows belonging to this batch.
                // Snapshot row k after pivots < k, before later pivots alter it.
                #pragma omp single
                {
                    for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
                        unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
                        std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes, numNodes, pivot);
                        for (size_t i = first; i < last; ++i) {
                            if (i == k) {
                                continue;
                            }
                            unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
                            updateDistanceRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot,
                                              row[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k),
                                              0, numNodes);
                        }
                    }
                } // The implicit barrier publishes all snapshots.

                #pragma omp for schedule(static)
                for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
                    if (i >= first && i < last) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
                    unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
                    unsigned int distToPivot[maxPivots];

                    // Update the pivot columns first, recording each distance
                    // at exactly the point the original k loop would read it.
                    for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
                        const unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
                        const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
                        distToPivot[k - first] = distIK;
                        updateDistanceRow(row, pathRow, pivot, distIK,
                                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k), first, last);
                    }

                    // Each destination tile stays hot through the whole batch.
                    // Preserve increasing k and strict comparisons, including
                    // the original path entries when distances tie.
                    for (size_t column = 0; column < numNodes;) {
                        if (column == first) {
                            column = last;
                            continue;
                        }
                        const size_t columnEnd = std::min(column + columnTile,
                            column < first ? first : numNodes);
                        for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
                            const unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
                            updateDistanceRow(row, pathRow, pivot,
                                              distToPivot[k - first],
                                              static_cast<unsigned int>(k),
                                              column, columnEnd);
                        }
                        column = columnEnd;
                    }
                } // Finish every source row before preparing the next batch.
            }
        }
    }
}
bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero'"\\n\", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n"'^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]'"\\n\", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);
    printf(\"Options:\\n\");
    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");
    printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);
    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, \"DistanceMatrix\");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#undef main
#include <random>
int main() {
    std::mt19937 rng(12345);
    size_t cases = 0;
    for (int threads : {1, 3, 8}) {
        omp_set_num_threads(threads);
        for (size_t n : {0, 1, 2, 3, 7, 31, 32, 33, 63, 64, 65, 127, 129}) {
            for (int mode = 0; mode < 3; ++mode) {
                std::vector<unsigned int> expected(n*n), expectedPath(n*n);
                if (mode == 0) {
                    reference_initializeDistanceMatrix(expected, n, 1, reference_MAX_DISTANCE);
                } else {
                    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
                        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
                            expected[i*n+j] = i == j ? 0 : (mode == 1 ? rng()%11 : (rng()%4 == 0 ? reference_INF : rng()%1000));
                        }
                    }
                }
                reference_initializePathMatrix(expectedPath, n);
                auto actual = expected;
                std::vector<unsigned int> actualPath(n*n);
                initializePathMatrix(actualPath, n);
                if (expectedPath "'!= actualPath) {
                    std::fprintf(stderr, "Initialization mismatch: n=%zu threads=%d'"\\n\", n, threads);
                    return 1;
                }
                reference_floydWarshall(expected, expectedPath, n);
                floydWarshall(actual, actualPath, n);
                if (actual "'!= expected || actualPath != expectedPath) {
                    for (size_t q = 0; q < n*n; ++q) {
                        if (actual[q] != expected[q] || actualPath[q] != expectedPath[q]) {
                            std::fprintf(stderr, "Mismatch: n=%zu threads=%d mode=%d cell=%zu dist=%u/%u path=%u/%u'"\\n\",
                                n, threads, mode, q, actual[q], expected[q], actualPath[q], expectedPath[q]);
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    return 1;
                }
                ++cases;
            }
        }
    }
    std::printf(\"PASS: exact distance and path equality in %zu cases\\n\", cases);
}

+                    // never improves. Keep it read-only for the whole team.
+                    if (i == k) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                    updateDistanceRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot, row[k],
+                                      static_cast<unsigned int>(k), 0, numNodes);
                 }
+                // The implicit barrier completes this pivot before the next.
             }
+        } else {
+            for (size_t first = 0; first < numNodes; first += pivotBatch) {
+                const size_t last = std::min(first + pivotBatch, numNodes);
+
+                // Prepare only the source rows belonging to this batch.
+                // Snapshot row k after pivots < k, before later pivots alter it.
+                #pragma omp single
+                {
+                    for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                        unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
+                        std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes, numNodes, pivot);
+                        for (size_t i = first; i < last; ++i) {
+                            if (i == k) {
+                                continue;
+                            }
+                            unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                            updateDistanceRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot,
+                                              row[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k),
+                                              0, numNodes);
+                        }
+                    }
+                } // The implicit barrier publishes all snapshots.
+
+                #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                    if (i >= first && i < last) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int distToPivot[maxPivots];
+
+                    // Update the pivot columns first, recording each distance
+                    // at exactly the point the original k loop would read it.
+                    for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                        const unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
+                        const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
+                        distToPivot[k - first] = distIK;
+                        updateDistanceRow(row, pathRow, pivot, distIK,
+                                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k), first, last);
+                    }
+
+                    // Each destination tile stays hot through the whole batch.
+                    // Preserve increasing k and strict comparisons, including
+                    // the original path entries when distances tie.
+                    for (size_t column = 0; column < numNodes;) {
+                        if (column == first) {
+                            column = last;
+                            continue;
+                        }
+                        const size_t columnEnd = std::min(column + columnTile,
+                            column < first ? first : numNodes);
+                        for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                            const unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
+                            updateDistanceRow(row, pathRow, pivot,
+                                              distToPivot[k - first],
+                                              static_cast<unsigned int>(k),
+                                              column, columnEnd);
+                        }
+                        column = columnEnd;
+                    }
+                } // Finish every source row before preparing the next batch.
+            }
         }
     }

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        unsigned int* row = path.data() + i * numNodes;
        #pragma omp simd
        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
            row[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(i);
        }
    }
}

// Every destination in a row is independent for a fixed pivot. The caller
// supplies an immutable pivot row and the source-to-pivot distance.
inline void updateDistanceRow(unsigned int* row, unsigned int* pathRow,
                              const unsigned int* pivot, const unsigned int distIK,
                              const unsigned int intermediate,
                              const size_t begin, const size_t end) {
    #pragma omp simd
    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
        if (newDist < row[j]) {
            row[j] = newDist;
            pathRow[j] = intermediate;
        }
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    unsigned int* distances = dist.data();
    unsigned int* paths = path.data();

    constexpr size_t maxPivots = 32;
    constexpr size_t columnTile = 256;
    const size_t maxThreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
    // Batching saves cache traffic for large row partitions. Smaller ones
    // benefit from direct pivots, without snapshotting or serial preparation.
    const bool useBatches = numNodes >= 4096 &&
        numNodes * numNodes / maxThreads >= (size_t{1} << 20);
    const size_t pivotBatch = useBatches
        ? std::min(maxPivots, std::max(size_t{1}, numNodes / (4 * maxThreads)))
        : 1;
    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotStorage(useBatches ? pivotBatch * numNodes : 0);
    unsigned int* pivots = pivotStorage.data();

    // Reuse one team for the entire computation. Static row ownership keeps
    // writes disjoint and makes successive updates reuse the same caches.
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        if (!useBatches) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int* pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
                #pragma omp for schedule(static)
                for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
                    // Nonnegative weights and a zero diagonal mean this row
                    // never improves. Keep it read-only for the whole team.
                    if (i == k) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
                    updateDistanceRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot, row[k],
                                      static_cast<unsigned int>(k), 0, numNodes);
                }
                // The implicit barrier completes this pivot before the next.
            }
        } else {
            for (size_t first = 0; first < numNodes; first += pivotBatch) {
                const size_t last = std::min(first + pivotBatch, numNodes);

                // Prepare only the source rows belonging to this batch.
                // Snapshot row k after pivots < k, before later pivots alter it.
                #pragma omp single
                {
                    for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
                        unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
                        std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes, numNodes, pivot);
                        for (size_t i = first; i < last; ++i) {
                            if (i == k) {
                                continue;
                            }
                            unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
                            updateDistanceRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot,
                                              row[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k),
                                              0, numNodes);
                        }
                    }
                } // The implicit barrier publishes all snapshots.

                #pragma omp for schedule(static)
                for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
                    if (i >= first && i < last) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
                    unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
                    unsigned int distToPivot[maxPivots];

                    // Update the pivot columns first, recording each distance
                    // at exactly the point the original k loop would read it.
                    for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
                        const unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
                        const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
                        distToPivot[k - first] = distIK;
                        updateDistanceRow(row, pathRow, pivot, distIK,
                                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k), first, last);
                    }

                    // Each destination tile stays hot through the whole batch.
                    // Preserve increasing k and strict comparisons, including
                    // the original path entries when distances tie.
                    for (size_t column = 0; column < numNodes;) {
                        if (column == first) {
                            column = last;
                            continue;
                        }
                        const size_t columnEnd = std::min(column + columnTile,
                            column < first ? first : numNodes);
                        for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
                            const unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
                            updateDistanceRow(row, pathRow, pivot,
                                              distToPivot[k - first],
                                              static_cast<unsigned int>(k),
                                              column, columnEnd);
                        }
                        column = columnEnd;
                    }
                } // Finish every source row before preparing the next batch.
            }
        }
    }
}

    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
+// Every destination in a row is independent for a fixed pivot. The caller
+// supplies an immutable pivot row and the source-to-pivot distance.
+inline void updateDistanceRow(unsigned int* row, unsigned int* pathRow,
+                              const unsigned int* pivot, const unsigned int distIK,
+                              const unsigned int intermediate,
+                              const size_t begin, const size_t end) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+        if (newDist < row[j]) {
+            row[j] = newDist;
+            pathRow[j] = intermediate;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
                    const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+    unsigned int* distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* paths = path.data();
+
+    constexpr size_t maxPivots = 32;
+    constexpr size_t columnTile = 256;
+    const size_t maxThreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
+    // Batching saves cache traffic for large row partitions. Smaller ones
+    // benefit from direct pivots, without snapshotting or serial preparation.
+    const bool useBatches = numNodes >= 4096 &&
+        numNodes * numNodes / maxThreads >= (size_t{1} << 20);
+    const size_t pivotBatch = useBatches
+        ? std::min(maxPivots, std::max(size_t{1}, numNodes / (4 * maxThreads)))
+        : 1;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotStorage(useBatches ? pivotBatch * numNodes : 0);
+    unsigned int* pivots = pivotStorage.data();
+
+    // Reuse one team for the entire computation. Static row ownership keeps
+    // writes disjoint and makes successive updates reuse the same caches.
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        if (!useBatches) {
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
+                const unsigned int* pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
+                #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                    // Nonnegative weights and a zero diagonal mean this row
+                    // never improves. Keep it read-only for the whole team.
+                    if (i == k) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                    updateDistanceRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot, row[k],
+                                      static_cast<unsigned int>(k), 0, numNodes);
                 }
+                // The implicit barrier completes this pivot before the next.
             }
+        } else {
+            for (size_t first = 0; first < numNodes; first += pivotBatch) {
+                const size_t last = std::min(first + pivotBatch, numNodes);
+
+                // Prepare only the source rows belonging to this batch.
+                // Snapshot row k after pivots < k, before later pivots alter it.
+                #pragma omp single
+                {
+                    for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                        unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
+                        std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes, numNodes, pivot);
+                        for (size_t i = first; i < last; ++i) {
+                            if (i == k) {
+                                continue;
+                            }
+                            unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                            updateDistanceRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot,
+                                              row[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k),
+                                              0, numNodes);
+                        }
+                    }
+                } // The implicit barrier publishes all snapshots.
+
+                #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                    if (i >= first && i < last) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int distToPivot[maxPivots];
+
+                    // Update the pivot columns first, recording each distance
+                    // at exactly the point the original k loop would read it.
+                    for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                        const unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
+                        const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
+                        distToPivot[k - first] = distIK;
+                        updateDistanceRow(row, pathRow, pivot, distIK,
+                                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k), first, last);
+                    }
+
+                    // Each destination tile stays hot through the whole batch.
+                    // Preserve increasing k and strict comparisons, including
+                    // the original path entries when distances tie.
+                    for (size_t column = 0; column < numNodes;) {
+                        if (column == first) {
+                            column = last;
+                            continue;
+                        }
+                        const size_t columnEnd = std::min(column + columnTile,
+                            column < first ? first : numNodes);
+                        for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                            const unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
+                            updateDistanceRow(row, pathRow, pivot,
+                                              distToPivot[k - first],
+                                              static_cast<unsigned int>(k),
+                                              column, columnEnd);
+                        }
+                        column = columnEnd;
+                    }
+                } // Finish every source row before preparing the next batch.
+            }
         }
     }
 }

+// Every destination in a row is independent for a fixed pivot. The caller
+// supplies an immutable pivot row and the source-to-pivot distance.
+inline void updateDistanceRow(unsigned int* row, unsigned int* pathRow,
+                              const unsigned int* pivot, const unsigned int distIK,
+                              const unsigned int intermediate,
+                              const size_t begin, const size_t end) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+        if (newDist < row[j]) {
+            row[j] = newDist;
+            pathRow[j] = intermediate;
+        }
+    }
+}

+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
                    const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];

-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+    unsigned int* distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* paths = path.data();
+
+    constexpr size_t maxPivots = 32;
+    constexpr size_t columnTile = 256;
+    const size_t maxThreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
+    // Batching saves cache traffic for large row partitions. Smaller ones
+    // benefit from direct pivots, without snapshotting or serial preparation.
+    const bool useBatches = numNodes >= 4096 &&
+        numNodes * numNodes / maxThreads >= (size_t{1} << 20);
+    const size_t pivotBatch = useBatches
+        ? std::min(maxPivots, std::max(size_t{1}, numNodes / (4 * maxThreads)))
+        : 1;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotStorage(useBatches ? pivotBatch * numNodes : 0);
+    unsigned int* pivots = pivotStorage.data();
+
+    // Reuse one team for the entire computation. Static row ownership keeps
+    // writes disjoint and makes successive updates reuse the same caches.
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        if (!useBatches) {
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
+                const unsigned int* pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
+                #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                    // Nonnegative weights and a zero diagonal mean this row
+                    // never improves. Keep it read-only for the whole team.
+                    if (i == k) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                    updateDistanceRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot, row[k],
+                                      static_cast<unsigned int>(k), 0, numNodes);
                 }
+                // The implicit barrier completes this pivot before the next.
+            }
+        } else {
+            for (size_t first = 0; first < numNodes; first += pivotBatch) {
+                const size_t last = std::min(first + pivotBatch, numNodes);
+
+                // Prepare only the source rows belonging to this batch.
+                // Snapshot row k after pivots < k, before later pivots alter it.
+                #pragma omp single
+                {
+                    for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                        unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
+                        std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes, numNodes, pivot);
+                        for (size_t i = first; i < last; ++i) {
+                            if (i == k) {
+                                continue;
+                            }
+                            unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                            updateDistanceRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot,
+                                              row[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k),
+                                              0, numNodes);
+                        }
+                    }
+                } // The implicit barrier publishes all snapshots.
+
+                #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                    if (i >= first && i < last) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int distToPivot[maxPivots];
+
+                    // Update the pivot columns first, recording each distance
+                    // at exactly the point the original k loop would read it.
+                    for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                        const unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
+                        const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
+                        distToPivot[k - first] = distIK;
+                        updateDistanceRow(row, pathRow, pivot, distIK,
+                                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k), first, last);
+                    }
+
+                    // Each destination tile stays hot through the whole batch.
+                    // Preserve increasing k and strict comparisons, including
+                    // the original path entries when distances tie.
+                    for (size_t column = 0; column < numNodes;) {
+                        if (column == first) {
+                            column = last;
+                            continue;
+                        }
+                        const size_t columnEnd = std::min(column + columnTile,
+                            column < first ? first : numNodes);
+                        for (size_t k = first; k < last; ++k) {
+                            const unsigned int* pivot = pivots + (k - first) * numNodes;
+                            updateDistanceRow(row, pathRow, pivot,
+                                              distToPivot[k - first],
+                                              static_cast<unsigned int>(k),
+                                              column, columnEnd);
+                        }
+                        column = columnEnd;
+                    }
+                } // Finish every source row before preparing the next batch.
             }
         }
